**1. Face Count Bridge**

There is no finished `faceCount ↔ bdry` bridge yet. The clean API should be built from:

`bdry_apply_eq_sum M f`
`tetContribution_apply` in [Eligible.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Eligible.lean:28)
`bdryGen_apply_mem_pm` in [Eligible.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Eligible.lean:33)
`tetContribution_apply_of_not_mem_tetFaces`, `tetContribution_ne_zero_of_mem_tetFaces`, `exists_erase_eq_of_mem_tetFaces` in [Theorem2.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Theorem2.lean:202)

Recommended new lemma:

```lean
lemma bdry_apply_mod_two_eq_faceCount_mod_two
    {M : Chain V} (hS : SimplicialChain M)
    (hPure : ∀ t ∈ M.support, t.card = 4)
    {f : Finset V} (hf : f.card = 3) :
    (bdry M f) % 2 = (faceCount M.support f : ℤ) % 2 := ...
```

Then derive:

```lean
lemma faceCount_eq_one_of_boundary_pseudomanifold
    {M : Chain V} {σ : Finset (Finset V)}
    (hS : SimplicialChain M)
    (hPure : ∀ t ∈ M.support, t.card = 4)
    (hU : UnitOn (bdry M) σ)
    (hPM : IsPseudomanifold M.support)
    {f : Finset V} (hfσ : f ∈ σ) :
    faceCount M.support f = 1 := ...
```

Proof: `hU.2 f hfσ` gives `(bdry M) f = ±1`, so odd mod 2. `hσ.pure` or `hU` plus sphere purity gives `f.card = 3`. `hPM f hf` gives `faceCount ≤ 2`; odd and ≤2 forces `=1`. This is not circular with Theorem 2, only uses the carried stickerball invariant.

**2. Triangle Rule-Out**

Target shape:

```lean
lemma faceCount_removeTet_sharedFace_eq_zero
    {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V} {t s : Finset V}
    (hσ : IsSphere2 σ) (hU : UnitOn X σ) (hMX : bdry M = X)
    (hT : IsTaut M) (hS : SimplicialChain M)
    (hNo3 : NoDegree3Vertex σ)
    (hPure : ∀ u ∈ M.support, u.card = 4)
    (ht : EligibleTet M t)
    (hs : s ∈ sharedFaces M t)
    (IHstick : ∀ σ' X' M', nrm M' < nrm M → ... → IsStickerball M'.support σ') :
    faceCount (removeTet M t).support s = 0
```

Key steps:

1. Assume `0 < faceCount (removeTet M t).support s`; get `w ∈ (removeTet M t).support` with `s ⊆ w`.
2. Use `support_removeTet_of_mem ht.2.1` to get `w ∈ M.support` and `w ≠ t`.
3. Since `s ∈ sharedFaces M t`, via `sharedFaces_eq_tetFaces_inter_sigma` or `hMX ▸ hU.1`, get `s ∈ σ`.
4. By the bridge above plus `IsPseudomanifold` of a smaller stickerball, `s` should be in exactly one tet in any smaller valid filling.
5. For contradiction, choose disjoint eligible pair from `aleph_disjoint_eligible_pair`. One of the two eligible tets has shared-face set disjoint from `sharedFaces M t`, hence misses `s`.
6. Call it `u`. Need `s ⊄ u`; if `s ⊆ u`, since `s.card = 3` and `u.card = 4`, `s ∈ tetFaces u`, and because `s ∈ σ = (bdry M).support`, this gives `s ∈ sharedFaces M u`, contradicting disjointness.
7. In `removeTet M u`, the tets `t` and `w` both remain and contain `s`. To get `≥ 3`, you need the parity bridge on `M`, not merely the two tets: boundary odd plus already two implies at least three.
8. Then `not_isPseudomanifold_of_faceCount` from [Pseudomanifold.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Pseudomanifold.lean:252) contradicts `IHstick ...` for `removeTet M u`.

Adversarial flag: the disjoint pair alone only gives a `u` whose shared faces avoid `s`. The `≥3` conclusion needs the mod-2 bridge. Without that bridge, the argument stops at `≥2`, which does not violate `IsPseudomanifold`.

**3. Edge Rule-Out**

Target:

```lean
lemma no_old_tet_on_shared_edge_after_removeTet
    {σ : Finset (Finset V)} {X M : Chain V} {t e : Finset V}
    ...
    (ht : EligibleTet M t)
    (he_shared : e = s₁ ∩ s₂) -- or `e ⊆ s₁ ∧ e ⊆ s₂`, `e.card = 2`
    :
    edgeLinkVerts (removeTet M t).support e = ∅
```

Use the existing engine:

`not_edgeLinkConnected_of_subset` in [Pseudomanifold.lean](/Users/doyle/Dropbox/taut/lean/Taut/Pseudomanifold.lean:260)

Structure:

1. Suppose some `w ∈ (removeTet M t).support` contains edge `ab`.
2. In the full `M.support`, the edge link of `ab` has the `cd` arc from `t` and a separate old component through `w`.
3. Pick `u` from `aleph_disjoint_eligible_pair` whose `sharedFaces` are disjoint from `sharedFaces M t`.
4. To apply `not_edgeLinkConnected_of_subset` to `(removeTet M u).support ⊆ M.support`, you need two apexes `x y ∈ edgeLinkVerts (removeTet M u).support ab` that are not reachable in `edgeLinkGraph M.support ab`.
5. `u` must not be the tet supplying either apex/component. Disjoint shared faces alone does not automatically prove this for an arbitrary old `ab`-link tet unless you package the link geometry so that any eligible tet hitting the separating bridge would force one of the two shared faces.

Adversarial flag: a pair probably suffices only if you prove a separate “eligible disjoint from the shared pair misses the bad `ab`-link separator” lemma. The pair theorem itself is not enough to establish the non-reachability witnesses survive deletion of `u`. Also avoid using Theorem 2/ball topology here; the allowed contradiction is only `IHstick → EdgeLinkConnected`, then `not_edgeLinkConnected_of_subset`.

**4. `theorem3_core` Restructure**

Make a parallel core carrying `IsStickerball`, not just `FreelyShellable`:

```lean
theorem theorem3_sticker_core
    (base : ... → IsStickerball M.support σ)
    (deg3_step : ... →
      (∀ σ' X' M', ... → IsStickerball M'.support σ') →
      IsStickerball M.support σ)
    (prime_step : ... →
      (∀ σ' X' M', ... → IsStickerball M'.support σ') →
      IsStickerball M.support σ)
    ... :
    IsStickerball M.support σ
```

Base: replace/augment `base_free` with `isStickerball_singleton`.

Degree-3: current `deg3_step_free` must additionally prove `IsPseudomanifold` and `EdgeLinkConnected` after inserting the star tet. Ideally use `isStickerball_insert`; this needs triangle clean and edge-link compatibility for the degree-3 star glue.

Prime case 1: after IH on `removeTet M e`, use existing free-shelling reassembly for `hfree`, then call `isStickerball_insert` with:

```lean
hpmc : ∀ f, f.card = 3 → f ⊆ e → faceCount (removeTet M e).support f ≤ 1
helc : ∀ a, a ⊆ e → a.card = 2 →
  edgeLinkVerts (removeTet M e).support a = ∅ ∨
  ((e \ a) ∩ edgeLinkVerts (removeTet M e).support a).Nonempty
```

The triangle and edge rule-outs supply the hard cases for the two shared faces / shared edge. Other faces/edges are easier from `exposedFaces`, no-flip geometry, and existing `FlipGeom` lemmas.

Prime case 2: the split package must carry `IsStickerball` for both sides from IH, not just `FreelyShellable`, and the bridge/reassembly must prove the union remains pseudomanifold and edge-link connected. This is extra work; `isStickerball_insert` only covers inserting one tet into one old stickerball, not gluing two stickerballs through the bridge unless you package it as sequential clean insertions.
